1b1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya badzra a dzi ta a na la pra mo ha nI TA ma d+hA ra NI/bod skad du/'phags pa rdo rje mi 'pham pa me ltar rab tu rmongs byed ces bya ba'i gzungs/

1b3(rgyud): ri sul gyi mchog/ri rab kyi smad dang rked ltar gser gyi rdo sna tshogs kyis gang zhing re'ur bres pa 'bar ba/rin po che'i rdo du ma'i 'od sna tshogs 'ja' lta bu byung ba'i rdo leb yangs pa/me 
1b4(rgyud): tog dri zhim po bkram zhing phye ma dang /bdug pa dri zhim pa mang pos phug gi nang dang nags bsgos pa/gdugs dang /a ga ru dang /phye ma dang /dril bu dang /bla res mdzes par byas 
1b5(rgyud): pa'i ri bo mchog la bzhugs te/de na bcom ldan 'das lha rnams dang /lha'i bu rnams dang /lha ma yin rnams dang /lha ma yin gyi bu rnams dang /rlung lha rnams dang /rlung lha'i bu 
2a1(rgyud): @#//rnams dang /nam mkha' lding rnams dang /klu rnams dang /gnod sbyin rnams dang /mi'am ci rnams dang /srin po rnams dang /dri za rnams dang /rig sngags 'chang grub pa rnams dang /'byung po rnams dang /log 'dren rnams dang /
2a2(rgyud): sha za rnams dang /snang ba dang /mi snang ba'i ma mo'i tshogs khyab par byed pa rgya chen po dag gis dpag tshad lnga brgyar 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du gyur par 'dus te/de na gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' bdun khri lnga stong dang /dge slong khri nyi stong lnga brgya dang 
2a3(rgyud): thabs cig go//bcom ldan 'das ni chos ston te/gnas par bde ba/mnyan na bde ba/'khor ba las nges par 'byung ba'i chos ston to//de na lha rnams dang /lha'i bu rnams dang /lha ma yin rnams dang /lha ma yin gyi bu rnams dang /rlung lha rnams 
2a4(rgyud): dang /rlung lha'i bu rnams dang /nam mkha' lding rnams dang /klu rnams dang /gnod sbyin rnams dang /mi'am ci rnams dang /srin po rnams dang /srin po'i bu rnams dang /dri za rnams dang /rig sngags 'chang grub pa rnams dang /'byung po rnams 
2a5(rgyud): dang /log 'dren rnams dang /sha za rnams dang /snang ba dang /mi snang ba'i ma mo'i tshogs rnams dang /'byung po'i tshogs rnams dang /'byud po'i tshogs rnams rnam par rgyu zhing ku co dang /'jigs pa dang nyam thag pa'i sgra 'byin to//bcom ldan 'das 
2a6(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shAkya thub pas sgra de dag gsan nas 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//kye bzhin bzangs dag ci'i phyir ku co dang /'jigs pa ba dang /nyam thag pa dang /'khrug pa'i sgra 'byin/de dag gis gsol 
2b1(rgyud): pa/bcom ldan 'das bdag cag gi dbang du bgyis te/gnod pa dang drang bas bkug cing drangs te tho btsams na/bcom ldan 'das bskyab tu gsol/de skad ces gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis thams cad kyi steng gi dkyil 'khor yangs pa ston pa'i mchog gi blo gros 
2b2(rgyud): dang /blo dang /las dang /las kyi mtha' dang ldan pa/sa chen po las sa gzhan thob pa rgyal tshab tu nye ste/chos kyi rgyal srid pa dbang bskur ba'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rjes su dgongs par mdzad pa tsam gyis 'ongs te/rdo rje'i zom ltar dpung mgo yangs pa/rdo 
2b3(rgyud): rje ltar mkhregs pa'i cod pan 'od zer 'tsher ba can/rdo rje'i dpung rgyan dang ldan pa/lus mkhregs shing sra ba dang /lag na rdo rje dang /zhags pa dang /lag na brtan po dbang ba thogs pa/rdo rje pur bu sra bas rab tu 'joms par byed pa/mthu chen po g.yog bzhi 'bum lnga khri dang 
2b4(rgyud): ldan pa/'jig rten gyi khams 'di kun du snang bar byed cing rdo rje'i rigs kyi gzhon nu dang /rdo rje'i sde dang /rdo rje'i be con dang /rdo rje'i snying po dang /rdo rje'i tog dang /rdo rje'i snang ba dang /rdo rje sngon po dang /rin po che'i rdo rje dang /gsang ba'i rdo rje dang /'khor lo rdo rje la sogs pa dang /rdo rje'i 
2b5(rgyud): dmag dpon chen po rnams dang /dmag skyong rnams dang /sa skyong rnams dang /rdo rje rig pa'i rgyal po yang dag pa dang /yang dag pa ma yin pa yang dag par rtogs pa rnams dang /rdo rje'i yul rnams dang /rdo rje rnam par 'dren rnams dang lhan cig tu rdo rje gzhon nu'i gdon rnams dang /
2b6(rgyud): rdo rje gnon po rnams dang /rdo rje rmi lam ngan byed rnams yang dag par skul/rnam par skul zhing 'khor gyi khyams chen por 'dus pa thams cad la kun du snang bar byed/rnam par snang bar byed/gsal bar byed/rnam par gsal bar byed/rab tu dga' bar byed/yang dag par 
2b7(rgyud): skul/so sor skul zhing bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste/bla gos phud nas zhabs la cod pan gyis gtugs te/lan gsum du bskor ba byas nas thal mo sbyar te phyogs gcig tu 'dug go/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis rjes su yi rang ba mdzad de/rdo rje 'chang khyod song la lha rnams

3b1(rgyud): rnams skrag par byed pa/srin po rnams spangs par byed pa/rang gi gsang sngags rnams rdzogs par byed pa/gzhan gyi gsang sngags rnams phur bus 'debs pa/nus pa rnams ston par byed pa/gsod par byed pa gsod pa/gnod par byed pa phung bar byed pa/de der 
3b2(rgyud): song nas lha rnams dang /lha'i bu rnams dbang du byed de/de ltar drag po pa drag po grub pa dang /khyab 'jug pa khyab 'jug grub pa dang /lha ma yin pa lha ma yin grub pa dang /de dag spyir skul zhing dkyil 'khor du 'jug/spyod par byed/mthu ston par byed/brjed par byed/

3b4(rgyud): byung ba'i 'od zer rnams kyis rdo rje dang bcas pa'i lag na rdo rje bskul nas/'od des de rab tu dga' ste rdo rje las snang ba bkye ba rnams kyis sa gsum gyi ston gyi skar ma dang /zla ba'i 'od zil du brlag par byas nas snang ba gcig pur byed cing rgya mtsho chen po ni skems/ri'i rgyal po ri 
3b5(rgyud): rab ni g.yo bar byed/glog gi 'khor lo dang /lce 'bebs pa'i 'brug ni bsgrags/sgra drag po ni byung /bsgyings pa'i sgra ni bsgrags/skrag par byas so//de na rdo rje mi 'pham pa me ltar/rab tu rmongs byed ces byas pa'i gzugs me lce 'od 'phro chen po khong na 'dug pa sgra tshogs 
3b6(rgyud): gser dang rin po che 'dra ba/rdo rje 'chang ba rdo rje'i bu mo zhes bya ba'i bcom ldan 'das ma des lan gsum bskor ba byas nas/yid las ches ma lcogs pa'i shugs 'od 'phro zhing 'bar ba lta bur drag pas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdzu 'phrul chen po la skyabs su song ngo //de 
3b7(rgyud): 'ongs pa dang /'phags pa rdo rje 'chang gis smras pa/skal ba chen po dge ma de bzhin gshegs pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis sems dang ldan ma/'di dag la lag cha dang mthun par bya ba'i stobs byin cig/de nas gzungs kyis smras pa/'di dag bka' stsal pa de bzhin du bgyi'o//
4a1(rgyud): @#//zhes lan gsum tshig tu smras so//thor tshugs gsum ma/sa gsum dang /dus gsum dang /drag po'i rtse gsum dang /khyab 'jug gi 'khor lo dang /gshin rje'i be con dang /lus ngan gyi dbyug to dang /dbang po'i rdo rje dang /DA D+ha'i zhags pa 
4a2(rgyud): dang /log 'dren rnams kyi bgegs dang /lha ma yin rnams kyi sgyu dang /rlung lha rnams kyi gzi dang /shugs dang /grub pa rnams kyi rmongs byed dang /ma mo'i tshogs rnams kyi gcod pa dang /klu rnams kyi dam tshig dang /dug dang /khro bo dang /gnod sbyin dang /srin 
4a3(rgyud): po dang /mi'am ci dang /sha za dang /'byung po dang /tshangs pa'i srin po dang /grul bum dang /bden bral dang /gnon po dang /ro langs kyi skabs su rgol ba rnams dang /de bzhin du thams cad kyang sbyongs shig/mig gi 'dzums pa tsam gyis yud tsam la thams cad mchod 
4a4(rgyud): nas lag na rdo rje la snying po 'di phul te smras pa/dkon mchog gsum la phyag 'tshal lo//gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon chen po lag na rdo rje drag po la phyag 'tshal lo//tad+ya thA/ta thA ga ta sa mA d+hi d+hA ra NI/ma hA a ba ra ta yi b+h+yA mi/a hi badzre/ku Ti le/ku Ti pA ba kA ku la shi Shi/ka pi la ke 
4a5(rgyud): shi/ta Ti tsi Ti ma Ta ni/karka Ta mu Ti ban+d+hi badzre/ma hA bid+hu l+lo lo le/bu gu bu lu/su mu su mu/sa ra sa ra/phaT phaT/su d+yo te/pi ga le tsi te/pi tsa Ti/dz+wa la pi ga le/sarba de bag Na/pra ha ra Na mA yA/sid+d+ha dA yi ke su ru ke/su ru ke/badzra g+ho Ne/badzra dr-i D+ha/s+pha ra pra ha ra Ne/
4a6(rgyud): rat+na tra ya/di kra la bud+d+he te/badzra d+ha ra bi shwa sa ni ka/a pra ti ha ta pra ma rda ni swA hA/gang gsang sngags 'di rjes su dran nas klog gam/sgom mam/'dzin pa de thos na ngan 'gro log par ltung bar 'gro bar mi 'gyur ro//'jig rten pa dang /'jig rten las 'das pa'i dkyil 
4a7(rgyud): 'khor thams cad la mchod pa byas par 'gyur ro//dam tshig dang phyag rgya la zhugs par 'gyur ro//bsam pa dang rjes su yi rang bas 'gugs par byed do//dug dang /gsang sngags dang /mdud pa dang /rdeg pa dang /'ching ba'i 'jigs pa la sogs rnam par skrag 
4b1(rgyud): par byed do//de nas lha dang /lha'i bu de dag gi de'i mthu la bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'chang de'i rnam par rol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa rnam pa mang po du ma yang myong ba nas steng gi gnas gtsang ma dang /'og min gyi lha dang /'jig rten gyi khams gzhan na yang 
4b2(rgyud): gnas pa dag gis 'od dang ldan pa'i me tog dang /bdug pa'i sprin dang rin po che mang po'i char rgya chen pos mchod nas bstod pa ldan pas bstod de/lhan cing zab la rnga sgra lta bu dang /bsil ba dang /bsod nams kyi tshigs su bcad pa dag kyang smras so//bcom ldan 'das kyis de 
4b3(rgyud): skad ces bka' stsal na/dge slong de dag dang /byang chub sems dpa' de dag dang /thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de dag mi rangs te bcom ldan 'das kyis gsung pa la mngon par bstod do//'phags pa rdo rje mi 'pham pa/me ltar rab tu rmongs byed ces bya ba'i gzungs 
4b4(rgyud): rdzogs so////rgya gar gyi mkhan po dzi na mi tra dang /dA na shI la dang /zhu chen gyi lo ts+tsha ba ban de ye shes sdes bsgyur cing zhus te skad gsar bcad kyis kyang bcos nas gtan la phab pa//@#//rgya gar skad du/badzra sUk+Sh+ma a 
4b5(rgyud): pra ti ha ta dza sar+b+ha ra NI/bod skad du/rdo rje phra mo thogs pa med pa zhes bya ba'i gzungs/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//bde gshegs khro bo thams cad la phyag 'tshal la//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das 
4b6(rgyud): rdo rje rigs pa'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//sangs rgyas kyi mthus gsang ba pa'i bdag po lag na rdo rje hU~M zhes bya bas bzhin rab tu 'bar ba bstan te/bcom ldan 'das kyi thad du 'dug go//de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs kyi sprul pa las bram ze'i khye'u cig 
4b7(rgyud): byung ste/gsang ba pa'i bdag po la 'di skad ces smras so//gsang ba pa'i bdag po/rig pa'i bdag po/gzungs kyi bdag po/lha'i gnas gsum po dag na lha nyam chung ba dang /klu nyam chung ba la zil gyis gnon pa drag pos rab tu gzhom pa'i gsang sngags dang /rig sngags 
5a1(rgyud): @#//gzungs sngags rnams rab tu brjod du gsol/lag na rdo rjes smras pa/bram ze'i khye'u rab tu nyan la yid la zung shig dang /nga yis bde gshegs khro bo thams cad kyis rtag tu brjod pa'i yang snying 'di rab tu smras so//na mo rat+na tra yA ya/na ma h+ya N+Da badzra pA 
5a2(rgyud): Na ye/ma hA yak+Sha se nA pa ta ye/tad+ya thA/oM ha ra ha ra badzra/ma tha ma tha badzra/d+hu na d+hu na badzra/da ha da ha badzra/pa tsa nga tsa badzra/d+ha ra d+ha ra badzra/dA ra ya dA ra ya badzra/dA ru Na dA ru Na badzra/ts+tshan+da ts+tshan+da badzra/b+hin+da b+hin+da badzra/hU~M phaT thA/na mish+tsaN+Da badzra kro d+hA ya/hu lu hu 
5a3(rgyud): lu|tiSh+Tha tiSh+Tha/ban+d+ha ban+d+ha/ha na ha na/a mr-i te hU~M phaT thA/bram ze'i khye'u gang la la zhig gis bde gshegs bdud rtsi 'khyil pa'i cha bzung ste/khro bo thams cad kyi rdo rje'i tshig 'di rab tu smra zhing /ba spu'i bu ga thams cad nas rdo rje phra mo grangs med pa sprul te phyogs bcur 
5a4(rgyud): spros na/sems can thams cad kyi bag chags ngan pa byang /tshe dang bsod nams rab tu rgyas te/dgra dang /dug dang /dus ma yin par 'chi ba las rnam par thar nas/gang zhig gis dmigs pa'i rgol ba ngan pa la rtsi ma gi ta sprin gyis bshig pa'am/skyu ru ra rlon 
5a5(rgyud): pa gser gyis gzhibs pa bzhin du dmigs te/rdo rje'i tshig 'di smra zhing /rdo rje phra mo spros pas de dag gi phung po lnga dbang po lnga myur du brlag par 'gyur ro//lhas smras na lha'i zla ba gcig gam/lo ldan lha ma yin mgo 'gas    par 'gyur ro/dge slong gis smras 
5a6(rgyud): na /bdud kyi mtshon cha rnams myur du btul bar 'gyur ro//mis brjod na gang la dmigs pa de mi'i zla ba gcig gam/mi'i lo ldan nges par brlag par 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das kyis lag na rdo rje dang /bram ze'i khye'u la legs so zhes bya ba byin nas mi snang bar gyur to/
5a7(rgyud): de nas bram ze'i khye'u des tshangs pa'i skad phyung ste/rdo rje'i tshig 'di lha gnas gsum por bsgrags so//rdo rje phra mo thogs pa med pa'i gzungs rdzogs so//@#//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya da sha badzra pA Ni hr-i da ya/bod skad du/

5b6(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyi bkas gsol ba thob shig/'di lta ste rgyal ba khyu mchog dang /'brog gnas dang /gangs gnas dang /lngas rtsen dang /nor bu bzang po dang /grags pa bzang po dang /lus ngan gyi bus mdzes byed dang /phreng ba mdzes dang /rgyal po chen po

6a2(rgyud): gi gsang sngags kyi gzhi rnams ni 'di dag yin te/tad+ya thA/u ge te/ke te/sang ka te/u b+hu le/d+ha d+he/ta to/ta pat+te swA hA/tsi tsa tsi tse/tsi khe lo ts+tshe da ni/b+he da ni/sar+b+ba bid+yA ts+tshe da ni/sarba kr-i ta/kA kho rde/pa ra kr-i ta nA sha ni/de la rig sngags kyi rgyal po lag na rdo rje 
6a3(rgyud): bcu po de dag gi gsang sngags kyi gzhi dam pa ni 'di rnams yin no//tad+ya thA/a le lak+She ba ke le yak+She/a ke/pa ke/la k+She swA hA/tad+ya thA/hu tu hu tu/du tu tu/bi tu bi tu/bi tu tu/gats+tshag ts+tsha/sa b+he/pa ta ha ta ye swA hA/na mo b+ha ga ba to bud+d+ha s+ya sid+d+h+yan+tu ma nA pa dA swA hA/bcom 
6a4(rgyud): ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal nas/tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo dang /lha dang /mi dang /lha ma yin dang /dri zar bcas pa'i 'jig rten yi rangs te/bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa la mngon par bstod do//'phags pa lag na rdo rje bcu'i snying po rdzogs so////
6a5(rgyud): rgya gar gyi mkhan po dzi na mi tra dang /dA na shI la dang /zhu chen gyi lo ts+tsha ba dan+de ye shes sdes bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab pa//@#//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya kuN+Da l+ya mr-i ta hr-i da ya/tsa tu r+tha nA ma d+hA ra NI/bod 
6a6(rgyud): skad du/'phags pa bdud rtsi thab sbyor gyi snying po bzhi pa zhes bya ba'i gzungs/dkon mchog gsum la phyag 'tshal lo//gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon chen po lag na rdo rje drag po la phyag 'tshal lo//de nas khro bo chen po bdud rtsi thab sbyor gyis 'khor 'dus pa thams cad la glang po che'i 
6a7(rgyud): lta stangs kyis bltas nas/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das 'di'i sems can rnams ni bsod nams dman pa dang /tshul la mi gnas pa dang /ba ma la ma gus pa dang /bsod nams chung ba dang /dad pa ma mchis pa dang /glags 
6b1(rgyud): tshol ba dang /rtag tu zang zing la brkam pa dang /rtag tu log 'dren dang /'tshe ba can dang /sha za rnams kyis zin pa legs te/bklags dang mig 'dren de dag gzhag pa dang /mtha' ba gtugs po 'dra skad du gsang sngags bshad par 'tshal lo//de nas bcom ldan 'das 
6b2(rgyud): kyis rdo rje khro bo la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//bdud rtsi thab sbyor gang khyod sems can thams cad la phan pa'i phyir zhus pa ni legs so legs so//khyod la de bzhin gshegs pas gnang gis sems can thams cad kyi don gyi phyir gsang sngags shod cig/de nas khro bo bdud rtsi thab sbyor 
6b3(rgyud): gyis/bcom ldan 'das dang /lag na rdo rje'i zhabs la gtugs nas gsang sngags bshad pa/na mo rat+na tra yA ya/na mash+tsaN+Da badzra pA Na ye/ma hA yak+Sha se nA pa ta ye/na mo badzra kro d+hA ya/prang+wa li ta dI p+ta daMSh+To t+ka Ta/b+ha ya b+hai ra bA ya/a si mu sa la/badzra ba rU pA sha ha stA ya/
6b4(rgyud): tad+ya thA/oM hu lu hu lu/a mr-i ta kuN+Da li ni/kha kha kha kha/kha na kha na/khA da khA da/khA hi khA hi/khA hi khA hi/tiSh+Tha tiSh+Tha/ha na ha na/da ha da ha/pa tsa pa tsa/gr-i h+na gr-i h+na/ban+d+ha ban+d+ha/gardza gardza/tardzi tardza/bi s+pho Ta ya/bi s+pho Ta ya/b+ha ga ban/a mr-i ta 
6b5(rgyud): kuN+Da li/mUr+dz+nyA na tA Da ya badzre Na/sarba big+h+nAn+bi nA yak+Sha na/ni pA ra ya/ma hA ga Na pa ti hrI bi tAn+ta/ka rA ya hUM phaT phaT phaT/oM kro d+ha ga Na pa ta ye swA hA/bdud rtsi thab sbyor gyi sngags dang po'o//na mo rat+na tra yA ya/na mash+tsaN+Da badzra pA Na ye/ma hA yak+Sha se nA pa ta ye/na mo

6b7(rgyud): tA yA ya/na mash+tsaN+Da badzre pA Na ye/ma hA yak+Sha sa nA ba ta ye/na mo badzra kro d+hA ya/bi ma la ka na ka dz+wa la b+hU Sha NA ya/ga ga na a mr-i tasam+b+ha bA ya/rak+ta mA l+ya/a nu le ba na pri yA ya/badzra pA Ni pri yA ya/badzra pA Ne tu l+ya/be gA ya/de ba/nA ga/yak+Sha/
7a1(rgyud): @#//rak+Sha sa/pra ma tha nA ya/trai lo kya go tsa rA ya/sha ta sai n+ya bi dra ba na ka rA ya/tri rat+na gan+d+hot+ta mA ya/a mr-i ta kuN+Da li/e h+ye hi diSh+Tota Ta/b+hai ra bA ya ga ga na bid+yud dz+wa la spu liM grA sha ni/tu l+ya ru bo d+hi taM saM b+hu ta/oM kha kha kha kha/gr-ih+Na gr-ih+Na/ban+d+ha ban+d+ha/ha 
7a2(rgyud): na ha na/gardza gardza/bi s+pho Ta ya/d+ha ka d+ha ka/dz+wa la dz+wa la/pra dz+wa la pra dz+wa la/gra sa gra sa/sarba big+hA ni nA ya kA nA nAM/hr-i da yaM phaT/gan+d+harba hr-i da yaM phaT/rak+Sha hr-i da yaM phaT/pra h+ma rak+Sha sa hr-i da yaM phaT/sarba dz+wa ra hr-i da yaM phaT/sarba tsA tu nA ka hr-i da yaM phaT/sarba nA gA tA na hr-i 
7a3(rgyud): da yaM phaT/hUM hUM phaT/da ha da ha/pa tsa pa tsa/A bi sha A bi sha/i da snu me kA r+yaM ku ru pa dz+wa d+ha ra A dz+wA bi ya ti swA hA/gsang sngags gsum pa'o//na mo badzra kro d+hA ya/diSh+Tota Ta/bai ra bA ya/tad+ya thA/hu lu hu lu/kha kha/khA hi khA hi/tiSh+Tha tiSh+Tha/ban+d+ha ban+d+ha swA hA/

21b2(rgyud): bon 'od rtse las//me tog sogs 'dzin lha mos mchod//gzugs sogs rdo rje ma rnams kyis//'dud cing so sor legs par mchod//thog ma med pa'i srid rgyud nas//lus ngag yid kyis spyad pa yi//sdig pa'i tshogs rnams ma lus kun//bgyis dang 
21b3(rgyud): bgyid du stsal ba dang //bgyid la rjes su yi rang ba//sdig pa'i tshogs rnamasam lus kun//thams cad so sor bshags par bgyi//slan chad mi bgyid sdom pa yang //rgya chen bdag gis gzung bar bya//rgyal ba sras bcas mdzad pa dang //'gro ba'i dge la rjes yi rang //
21b4(rgyud): rgyal dang byang chub sems dpa' dang //rigs ldan rdo rje lha mo dang //sngags rgyud rgya chen 'khor lo la//kun nas dang bas skyabs su mchi//mi rtog blo gros bskyed pa'i phyir//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang //gdul bya ji srid tshim pa ru//mya ngan 
21b5(rgyud): mi 'da' gsol ba 'debs//rnam pa kun gyi mchog ldan pa'i//byang chub sems ni bskyed par bya//gdod nas ji srid da lta yi//bar du lus 'dis ci ma byas//da ni srid pa'i rlung rtogs nas//rang gi lus 'di dbul bar bgyi//theg chen bla na med pa yi//lam 
21b6(rgyud): chen rgya mtsho gang yin pa//theg chen 'bras bu thob par du//rgyun mi chad par bsten par bya//de ltar dang sems 'dun byas te//bsod nams rgya chen rnams bsags pa'i//dge ba bla na med pa de//thams cad byang chub phyir bsngo'o//dang po byams pa'i 'od 
21b7(rgyud): zer gyis//'gro rnams bde la dgod par bya//gnyis pa snying rje'i 'od zer gyis//'gro ba sdug bsngal 'bral sems bskyed//gsum pa dga' bas bde ba thob//bzhi pa chags bral btang snyoms so//de rjes rags pa'i stobs spong zhing //bag chags 
22a1(rgyud): @#//dri ma bsal nas kyang //nam mkha' lta bur sgrub byed pa'i//de nas dag pa rjes dran bya//oM swa b+hA ba shud d+hAHsarba d+harmAHswa b+hA ba shud d+ho& hUM/de yis dag par byas nas ni//stong nyid rjes su bsgom par bya//oM shU n+ya tA dz+nyA na badzra swa b+hA ba At+ma 
22a2(rgyud): ko& haM/dngos po thams cad ma lus par//nam mkha' lta bur bsgom par bya//der ni ra~M skyes nyid kyi 'khor//dmar po 'od zer 'khrigs pa bsgom//de dbus hU~M yig sngon po yi//'od kyis 'gro ba'i bdud bsal te//slar yang yi ge la gzhug 
22a3(rgyud): par//rdo rje khro mo yongs su rdzogs//zhal gsum phyag drug me phung bzhugs//thugs kar nyi mar hU~M sngon bsam//'od kyis bzung ba'i 'khor lo bya//shar du rdo rje lhor dbyug pa//nub tu chu ste byang du mda'//byang shar rtse gsum shar lho me//lho nub ral 
22a4(rgyud): gri nub byang rlung //'og tu sna tshogs rdo rje brag/steng phyogs rdo rje rtse lnga'i gur//gsang sngags brjod pas bsgom par bya//hU~M las 'od zer rab spros pas//'khor lo bkod pa'i phyi rol tu//rdo rje rtse gsum ra ba dang //gur ni rab tu bsgom par bya//de rjes 
22a5(rgyud): rdo rje khro mo ni//zhu nas rang bzhin med par gyur//de las nyi ma'i steng gnas pa'i//bdud bzhi mnan cing nyi zla ni//A li kA li las byung ba'o//bar du hU~M las rdo rje ni//khro mo'i rang bzhin 'od kyis bskor//khro mo dang bcas 'od zer rnams//rdo rje la 
22a6(rgyud): bzhugs sku bzhengs te//zhal drug phyag drug zhabs drug gam//yang na zhabs bzhis bdud bzhi mnan//zhal kun sngo zhing khro gnyer can//dmar ser phyin skra thod phreng can//rdo rjes lus brgyan nam mkha'i mdog/bcu gnyis phyag ste g.yas pa na//rdo 
22a7(rgyud): rje zhags pa mda' gsor zhing //sbrul dang dgra sta gri gug 'dzin//g.yon pa dril bu sdigs mdzub gzhu//dbyig pa rtse gsum tho ba'o//yang na phyag drug g.yas dang pos//rdo rje gnyis pas lcags sgrog ste//gsum pas mda' ni gsor bar byed//
22b1(rgyud): g.yon gyi dang pos sdigs mdzub ste//gnyis pa sbrul gyi zhags pa'o//gsum pas gzhu ste 'gengs pa'i tshul//rdo rje lu gu rgyud ma yi//bsam gtan ting 'dzin la gnas na//yon tan kun gyi bdag nyid kyi//sngags kyi rtsa bar 'gyur ba'o//
22b2(rgyud): sngags kyi bdag nyid yongs bskyed cing //lha yi rang bzhin bsgrub pa yi//rin chen gzungs kyi phreng bas ni//chos rnams mtha' dag thob 'gyur te//ral gri mig sman ril bu dang //rkang pa byug dang gter chen dang //sa 'og 'gro dang bcud len dang //nam 
22b3(rgyud): mkhar 'gro dang gser 'gyur dang //de bzhin sa gzhan 'gro ba dang //zhi dang rgyas dang dbang mngon spyod//rmongs dang rengs dang bskrad pa dang //dbye dang dgug dang rig sngags 'ching //bsrung dang phyir bzlog chen po dang //zhwa dang lkugs dang sems 
22b4(rgyud): brgyal dang //long dang sna tshogs nad skyed pa'i//sbyor ba rgya chen 'grub pa dang //mngon shes drug po yongs skye zhing //sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje yi//phyag rgya chen po thob 'gyur na//ltas mthong tsam la sogs pa yi//dngos grub phra mo smos ci dgos//
22b5(rgyud): sngags kyi sbyor ba bskyed pa'i lha//dmigs pa'i rtsa bar gyur pa yi//hU~M las skyes pa'i lha mo ni//sngon mo zhal gsum phyag drug ma//g.yas pa ser zhing g.yon pa dmar//spyan gsum sdang mig khro gnyer can//dmar ser gyen sgra thod pas 
22b6(rgyud): brgyan//rus pa'i rgyan gyis spras shing 'gying //thod pa'i phreng can klu yis brgyan//zhabs gnyis brgyad bskum ro la brdzis//phyag drug g.yas kyi dang po na//rdo rje gnyis pas gri gug ste//gsum pa sbrul gyi zhags pa 'dzin//g.yon gyi dang po 
22b7(rgyud): sdigs mdzub ste//gnyis pas thod pa gsum pa na//sbrul gyi zhags pa dkar po'o//yang na zhal kun sngo bsangs nyid//'dis ni 'ching dang dbang gi las//mthar dag rdzogs par byed pa'o//srid par bram ze skyes lha mo//sngon mo zhal gcig phyag 
23a1(rgyud): @#//gnyis ma//phyag g.yas rdo rje g.yon lcags sgrog/gcer bu rus pa'i rgyan ldan pa//bsad pa'i las rnams thams cad ni//rdo rje rnal 'byor ma 'dis bsgrub//de nas bcom ldan rdo rje can//chos rnams thams cad 'byung byed cing //dngos grub 
23a2(rgyud): myur du skyed pa yi//phyag rgya 'byung zhes bya ba yi//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms zhugs nas//yan lag phyag rgya bka' stsal pa//lag gnyis thal mo sbyar byas shing //slar la khu tshur bcad byas te//rgyab gnyis sprad nas mdzub mo dang //mthe'u chung lcags 
23a3(rgyud): sgrog bcing byas te//kha yi thad kar bzhag nas ni//rig pa'i phreng ba brjod pa yis//'dod pa thams cad 'grub par byed//phyag rgya bcings nas gsang sngags ni//rjes su dran par byas tsam gyis//lam du 'gro yang bde bar thar//gzhan gyi rig sngags 
23a4(rgyud): gcod par byed//'phags ma rdo rje lu gu rgyud ma'i rgyud kyi rig sngags las lha dang phyag rgya gtan la dbab pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////
23b1(rgyud): sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje yis//rnam par spras pa'i mtshan ma la//mtshan ma med pa rnams gnas pa//shes nas mtshan nyid rnam par dag/oM sarba ta thA ga ta dz+nyA na badzra swa b+hA ba At+ma ko&haM/gang zhig dbang pos yul mtshon pa//mtshon bya mtshan nyid 
23b2(rgyud): rnam shes dang //ye shes nyid ni mngon sum du//skye ba nyid la the tshom med//de nas rgya chen sbyor ba'i gtso//'khor lo'i cho ga bshad par bya//gro ga ras sam shing shun la//gur gum gi waM tsan+dan bcas//bu ka ser pos 'khor lo ni//rtsibs bcu 
23b3(rgyud): pad+ma'i steng du bri//mtha' nas me yi phreng bas bskor//pad+ma'i 'dab ma bcu la ni//phyogs bcu kun du bcing ba yi//rdo rje la sogs bcu po bri//de yi rgyab tu phyogs bcu rnams//bcing ba'i sngags ni rim bzhin bri//'khor lo'i rtsibs rnams bcu po 
23b4(rgyud): la//hUM gnyis bar du bsgrub bya'i ming //bsrung ba dang bcas bri bar bya//phyogs bzhir sngags dang phyag rgya dang //'khrul 'khor gcod dang 'ching ba dang //'gugs pa'i sngags su bcas pa bri//gzhan yang mtshams rnams drug por ni//lha dang klu dang lha min gyi//gnod 
23b5(rgyud): sbyin grul bum la sogs pa//bcing pa drug po bri bar bya//lta bar bsgrub bya'i gzugs brnyan ni//skyil krung thugs kar thal mo sbyar//mdzes pa'i gos ldan bri bar bya//de rjes gsang sngags phreng ba nyid//stong phrag gcig tu brjod byas nas//de rjes bstod 
23b6(rgyud): pas bstod byas te//gser sogs dril nas lus sam mgul//gang bde'i gnas su gdags par bya//des ni rnal 'byor bsrung don du//ji ltar bsam pa kun 'grub ste//'jigs pa brgyad po med par byed//de nas gzhan yang rgyal po'i ras//gsar 
23b7(rgyud): la tsan+dan ga pur dang //dri zhim rnams kyis byas nas ni//rgyu skar rgyal gyi nyi ma la//gur gum gyis ni 'khor lo bri//dbus su pad+ma 'dab brgyad pa'i//phyi rol mu khyud gsum ldan pa'i//pad+ma'i lte bar bsgrub bya'i gzugs//yan lag rdo rjes mtshan pa bri//de la 
24a1(rgyud): @#//snying po'i sngags kyis bskor//de rjes lte ba'i mu khyud la//steng 'og bcing ba'i sngags kyis bri//phyogs mtshams sngags rnams 'dab ma la'o//phyi rol mu khyud dang por ni//rtsa ba'i sngags ring dgod par bya//gnyis par 'od kyi ra ba bri//dri zhim 
24a2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor steng bzhag ste//rang nyid lha yi sbyor rdzogs bya//thugs ka'i sa bon 'od kyis ni//nam mkhar 'khor lo sbyangs byas te//yi ge hUM gi rnam par bsam//de yongs gyur las sku tshogs sgom//de dbus shes rab rnam par bsam//zla dbus steng du hUM las 
24a3(rgyud): ni//'khor lo rtsibs ldan bsgom par bya//der ni yi ge'i rnam pa ni//mar me lta bu gsal dmigs la//mkha' 'gro ma dang dbyer med bya//rang snying sa bon las byung ba'i//gsang sngags stong phrag bzlas byas nas//gser sogs dril nas lus sam mgul//
24a4(rgyud): btags na 'jigs pa brgyad las skyob//thams cad du ni bsrung par des//yungs kar ba yi rnam lnga dang //dUr ba'i snye mar bcas pa la//gsang sngags stong phrag zlas nas ni//rgan mo ming med sbyor ba yis//gnas gsum thig le byas bsrung na//tshe ni stong phrag 
24a5(rgyud): du mar 'phel//ku sha ba mar yungs kar til//gsang sngags stong phragabazlas byas te//shis par gyur pa'i nyi ma la//mchod rten spyi bor gdags byas na//'go ba'i nad rnams 'chad par 'gyur//khrag dang khro chur bcas pa la//gsang sngas stong phrag 
24a6(rgyud): gsum bzlas te//zla ba gzas gzung dus su ni//nye bsnyen rdzogs par byas nas ni//rdzas ni shin tu snga byas te//lha tshogs gnod pa gang dmigs pa'i//snying gar sngags ni bsnun byas na//gang dmigs bgegs ni rnam par 'jig/ras bal gsar bar 
24a7(rgyud): gyur pa la//shes rab mtshan ldan khrus byas te//shis pa'i nyi ma la bkal nas//bcings pa'i grangs kyis mdud byas te//stong phrag gcig gi sngags bzlas nas//lus sam mgul lam spyi bor gdags//des ni tshe lo stong phrag thub//yang na rang 
24b1(rgyud): lus rnams bsgrims nas//chu yi tshogs kyis dag byas te//nyi shu rtsa gcig grangs ldan par//gzungs kyi phreng ba brjod byas na//gser phyag lag tu ma blangs shing //bden bral phyi rol ma song bar//gzhan gyi 'jigs pa ma bsal zhing //che lo 
24b2(rgyud): brgya ni rtsa brgyad par//rims nad tsam yang mi gnod do//gos dang zas dang snod spyad dang //sman la sogs pa'i rdzas rnams ni//mi 'khrugs rgyal po'i phyag rgya yis//bsrungs na cis kyang mi shod de//seng chen rngam pa'i stabs kyis ni//'ug pa'i sgra gsum 
24b3(rgyud): brjod byas na//phyi rol rig sngags stong phrag ni//du mas cis kyang mi tshugs so//klu yi dbang po gnas lugs kyis//rtse gcig rdo rje'i phyag rgya bcas//de nas gsang sngags gsum brjod na//nad dang gdon la sogs pa sel//ri bong nu ma'i 
24b4(rgyud): mtshams byas nas//hU~M gis mnan pa'i sngags kyis ni//dmigs med ting 'dzin yid brtan zhing //'dis kyang gnod pa las bsrung 'gyur//phyi rol sogs pa kun spangs nas//snang ba yid kyis bslabs byas te//mi snang ba la mngon dmigs nas//bsrung ba 
24b5(rgyud): rnams kyi dam pa bshad//de nas gzhan yang sbyor ba bshad//dur khrod la sogs 'jigs gnas sam//mtsho dang mtshe'u dang dgon gnas dang //gnas dang skyed mos tshal chen dang //me tog tshal lam yul 'khor ljongs//chu yi 'gram mam shing gcig gam//
24b6(rgyud): yang na rang gi khyim nyid du//maN+Dal legs par byugs pa la//brten nas lha yi sbyor ba sgrub//ras ris la sogs mdun byas te//bde ba'i stan la skyil krung gyis//bsdad nas mnyam gzhag brtsam par bya//tha mal sbyangs pa'i lus kyis ni//thar pa 
24b7(rgyud): chen po thob 'gyur te//gzhan du de ltar ma yin no//sa gzhi shin tu nyams dga' zhing //legs par bkod pa'i ri bo la//nags tshal shin tu sdug phal//bsil ba'i chu rgyun 'babs pa'i phyogs//rnam bkod me tog tshal gseb tu//shar du sgo bstan 
25a1(rgyud): khang pa la//sngags bzhis mtshams bcings cho ga yis//gsal khung dang bcas brtsigs pa'i phyogs//snyan pa'i bya yis sgra sgrogs shing //dri zhim spos dad ldan pa la//dril bu sil sil sgra snyan sgrogs//lda ldi gos kyi phreng bas dkris//lhag 
25a2(rgyud): pa'i 'du 'dzi rnam spangs nas//spyod pa mthun pa'i grogs dang ni//bde bar byang chub bsgrub par bya//'on kyang gnas dang grogs la sogs//rnal 'byor yid 'jug phyir brtsam ste//mtha' dag spangs pa'i pha rol na//lhag pa'i byang chub ma mchis so//bde dang sdug bsngal 
25a3(rgyud): sna tshogs rnams//rang gi sems las 'byung mod kyi//sems rtog ma lus spang ba las//ngas ni chos gzhan 'ga' ma bshad//de bas sems rtog sna tshogs rnams//spang bar bya phyir sna tshogs thabs//sngags dang phyag rgya ting 'dzin dang //sbyor ba rgya chen rnams bshad 
25a4(rgyud): do//drag po sgrub pa'i thabs 'ga' ni//rig pa'i khro mos mnyan par gyis//sa gzhi bdud rtsi lnga yis byugs//me yi dkyil 'khor sa bon bcas//dur khrod pha gus bri bar bya//der ni pho mo thod pa la//phyi rol dmar nag tshon gyis byugs//nang du sna tshogs 
25a5(rgyud): rdo rje ni//mu khyud gsum ldan bri bar bya//dbus su rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma'i//snying po sngags bcas bri bar bya//rdo rje rwa ni thams cad la//hU~M bri mu khyud gnyis phreng sngags//gsum par khro bo'i sngags bri'o//thod pa kha sbyor nang du ni//ske tshe yungs kar btsan dug 
25a6(rgyud): dang //lan tshwa khrag dang sha chen dang //rkang rjes sa la sogs pa blugs//gcer bu rnal 'byor skra bshig nas//lha yi sbyor ldan lhor bltas nas//zhabs kyis thod pa'i 'khor lo ni//mnan te sngags phreng brjod par bya//khro bo chen po'i sbyor ba yi//
25a7(rgyud): mnyam bzhag nas ni stod phrag tu//bzlas pa mtshan phyed par byas na//bsad pa nyid la the tshom med//dur khrod ras la 'khor lo ni//dbus su me yi dkyil 'khor can//rtsibs brgyad mu khyud gsum ldan pa//dbus su snying po sngags rnams la//hU~M 
25b1(rgyud): gnyis bar du bsgrub bya'i ming //bsad pa nyid dang ldan par bya//mu khyud gnyis pa phreng sngags kyang //ming dang bsad par ldan pa'o//gsum pa khro bo'i sngags bris te//'khor lo mchod cing byin brlabs nas//dgra gzugs sor brgyad snying gar gzhug/
25b2(rgyud): de nyid til mar gyis byugs te//thod pa gnyis kyi 'khor lo yi//bar du bcug nas me steng gzhag/seng ldeng phur pa sor brgyad pa//mnyam gzhag sbyor bas sngags de gdab//bsad pa'i sbyor ba 'di nyid kyis//shi bar 'gyur la gzhan du min//yang na 
25b3(rgyud): byang chub yan lag las//dur khrod khrag gis 'khor lo bri//gru gsum brtsegs pa'i gzugs bris te//dbus su dgra ming gsang sngags bcas//rtsibs la bcu gnyis bar du ni//ming dang bsad pa bcas pa bri//mu khyud dang po'i sngags phreng yang //rgyas 
25b4(rgyud): pa'i yan lag ldan par bri//spra tshil gzugs brnyan sor brgyad pa'i//snying gar bcug nas skra yis dkri//rnal 'byor pa yi nyal ba'i zhabs//mgo bo lhor bstan gzhag par bya//chu shel ma mthong bar du ni//nyi shu rtsa gcig sngags brjod nas//mi yi rdo 
25b5(rgyud): rje'i dar gyis brgyan//sdang byed sa steng 'dir mi gnas//klu yi gnas su grags pa'am//chu yi rdzing la sogs 'gram du//dkyil 'khor ba yi rnam lngas byugs//pad+ma dmar po'i ri mo la//lte ba rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma//gser gyi mdog can thal mo sbyar//
25b6(rgyud): klu yi gdengs ka bdun ldan pa//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor steng gnas pa//shin tu bltas pas gnas par bya//dga' bo nyer dga' 'jog po dang //mtha' yas dung skyong rigs ldan gzugs//pad+ma pad chen rnams byas te//dkar dang ser dang ljang ba dang //
25b7(rgyud): dmar dang ljang skya sngo skya dang //dmar ser ljang ser 'dzum pa'i gzugs//gtso la thal mo sbyar btud nas//rgyu skar phreng ba dang ldan gnas//'o ma 'bras can mkhal zho sha//pus ka tsa ti la sogs sman//khur ba tshogs bu dang bcas pa//rin chen 
26a1(rgyud): @#//snod du dgram par bya//gza' dang rgyu skar rnams dga' zhing //klu rnams bde ba'i sems thob shog/lha rnams yid ni mnyam gzhag nas//snying rje'i blo chen skye bar shog/lha klu gnod sbyin dri za sogs//byang chub sems chen dang ldan nas//
26a2(rgyud): thugs rje nye bar dgongs nas kyang //dus su char chen 'beb par shog/smon lam de yi rjes su ni//gzungs kyi phreng ba brgya rtsa brgyad//brjod pa'i de ma thag tu ni//char gyi rgyun yang 'bab par 'gyur//gal te cung zad mi 'bebs na//mel bsdigs pa tsam 
26a3(rgyud): gyis ni//glog gi phreng ba gtor byed na//char chen 'bebs la the tshom med//wa yi khrag gis grog la//gang gi 'khor lo bskrad pa yi//thabs dang ldan par bri byas nas//dur khrod sa las byas pa yi//rnga mo'i snying gar bcug nas kyang //rkang pa 
26a4(rgyud): g.yon pas mnan nas ni//lhor bltas bka' yi sbyor ba yis//gsang sngags khri phrag gcig brjod na//bskrad pa nyid la the tshom med//sbrul dang sre mo'i khrag gis ni//rta dang ma he'i pags pa la//mnyam du gnyis kyi gzugs bri te//dbye ba'i sngags dang 
26a5(rgyud): bcas pa yi//'khor lo thod pa'i gzugs bris la//pags pa gnyis kyis dkris byas pa//zla gam bye brag dkyil 'khor la//brten pas dbye ba'i sbyor ba rdzogs//mtshal dang tsan+dan ga pur bsres//zla gam dbyibs kyi dkyil 'khor la//snying po dbang 
26a6(rgyud): gi sbyor bar bcas//nub tu bltas nas bsgrubs pa yi//lha yi dbang po dbang du 'gyur//rgyud kyi khyad par sbyor ba'o//dri yi rgyal po sna tshogs kyis//shing gi lo ma dkar po la//rdo rje rgya gram gnyis brtsegs kyi//phyogs kyi rwa rnams hU~M gis spras//
26a7(rgyud): mtshams kyi rwa rnams dbu ma la//hU~M bri rwa yi yan lag la//phaT ni phyir la bltas pa bri//rgya chen gsol ba gdab par bya//gos bzang ngam ni skags shing du//btsug nas rgyal mtshan rtser btags na//rlung dang thog dang ser ba gcod//dman pa rnams 
26b1(rgyud): dang 'brel ba spang //thal ba chu dang yungs kar la//sngags kyi phreng ba bzlas byas te//sprin la sogs pa 'khrugs pa'i tshe//gtor ba tsam gyis 'chad par 'gyur//'bru mar yungs kar ser po la//sngags nas lus la byugs tsam gyis//phran tshogs nad rnams 
26b2(rgyud): thams cad las//ma he shin tu brtan par byed//'phags ma rdo rje lu gu rgyud kyi 'khrul 'khor gyi le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba//sbyin sreg thab kyi sbyor ba yis//dngos grub thams cad myur byed de//sna tshogs lha mos mnyan par 
26b3(rgyud): byos//ser sna spangs shing g.yo sgyu med//dul la dpa' brtul bzod pa che//mkhas shing rig pa brtson 'grus bzang //sngags la mkhas pas sbyin sreg brtsam//thab ni nyi shu sor tshad la//phyed brkos kha phyir rdo rje ni//rtse gsum dur khrod tshon gyis bri//

162b6(rgyud): po la//me tog phreng ba dkar pos brgyan//ras phran dkar po dpyangs pa gzung //slob ma yon ni cher phul zhing //bla ma bsnyen bskur byas pa dag/stan la de ni bzhag nas su//sngags kyis skyon bsal bkra shis bya//bdug pa legs par sbyar 
162b7(rgyud): ba yi//bdug pa khro bo'i rgyal pos bdug/rnga yab la sogs gzung ba dang //spyi bo steng du gdugs kyang gzung //rnga ma'i bsil yab bdug spos dang //zhwa dang gos kyis slob ma dag pa//'di dag dang ni de bzhin du//bum pa bzhi yang bshams te dgod//
163a1(rgyud): @#//shin tu bkra shis tshigs bcad bsgrags//de bzhin bkra shis rgya cher gdon//'byor par 'gyur ba'i sil snyan dang //glud byangs dag dang rnga sgra bsgrags//mdor na de la sogs pa yi//cho ga rgyus pa byas nas su//yid kyis 
163a2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor lha kun la//mkhas pas gsol ba btab nas ni//bum pa bzlas brjod brgyar byas la//de ni legs par blangs nas su//dkyil 'khor dal bus lan gsum bskor//rtsa ba'i gsang sngags kun 'don cing //blo dang ldan pas dbang 
163a3(rgyud): bskur bya//de nas phyag rgya bcings nas su//gsang sngags blos ni spyi bos gtugs//gsang sngags rgyud ni 'dzin 'gyur ba//blo dang ldan pas dbangabaskur ro//slob ma yon tan kun ldan pa//nub phyogs kha bltas cho ga bya//byang phyogs kha bltas 
163a4(rgyud): zhi ba'i phyir//rgyas pa'i cho ga shar du bltas//drag shul spyod par de bzhin du//lho phyogs kha bltas brtson de bya//de ltar dbang bskur byas nas ni//de la sar pa'i gos rnams bskon//me tog dri la sogs pas mchod//mchod phyir thogs dang 
163a5(rgyud): gdu bur bcas//de nas bla ma bdag nyid kyis//de yi spyi bor gdugs kyang gzung //thal mo sbyar ba byas nas ni//dkyil 'khor legs par bskor du gzhug/gdugs dang bcas pas sgo phyogs su//spyi bo btud de phyag 'tshal gzhug/gsang 
163a6(rgyud): sngags rgyud ni 'dzin 'gyur pa'i//slob ma 'di ni gus pa lags//mkhan pos de skad smras nas su//gdugs bzhag nas ni slob ma de//lag pas legs par blangs nas su//de nas 'di skad bsgo bar bya//da ni dkyil 'khor slob dpon 
163a7(rgyud): 'gyur//gsang sngags rgyud kyang 'dzin par 'gyur//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' dang //lha rnams kyang ni kun gyis bkur//sems can rnams la snying brtse'i phyir//khyod kyis dkyil 'khor cho ga bzhin//nan tan bskyed de bri bar byos//
163b1(rgyud): sgrub pa po yang rgyud la sbyor//de skad bsgo nas de 'og tu//de nas slob ma de yi phyir//sbyin sreg cho ga sngon bzhin bya//yam sreg shing gi me la ni//dkyil 'khor bdag po'i gsang sngags kyis//mar gyi sbyin sreg brgya dag bya//de bzhin zhi ba'i 
163b2(rgyud): gsang sngags kyis//zho dang sbrang rtsi mar sbyar ba'i//til gyi sreg blugs brgya yang bya//sbyin sreg zin nas spyi bor ni//chu de nyid kyis bsang gtor bya//de nas de la dkyil 'khor ni//rab tu rgyas par bstan par bya//phyag rgya chen po dag kyang bstan//
163b3(rgyud): rig sngags rgyal po klog tu gzhug/bla mas de la ci nus par//cho ga bzhin du zlos su gzhug/de nas slob ma blo ldan pas//me tog snyim pa bkang nas su//bdag gis sngags dang lha rnams kyang //go rims bzhin du mchod byas nas//slob dpon 
163b4(rgyud): sbyin sreg gnas 'dug la//dga' zhing gus pas dran byas la//de tshe bla ma ci 'dod pa'i//yon ni phyag rgyas dbul bar bya//bdag dang bdog pa kun bcas par//slob dpon la ni dbul bar bya//bdag nyid dbang ba'i skye bo kun//nor dang bcas shing 
163b5(rgyud): rkang 'gros bcas//gzhan yang nor gyis mgu ba'am//yang na gang la dga' yang rung //yang na bdag la ci 'dod pa//bla ma la ni de dbul lo//yang na dbul pos bla ma la//bkur sti dag gis mgu bar bya//slob dpon dag ni thams cad la//bkur 
163b6(rgyud): sti bya ba yon du bshad//dang por ras zung gcig phul nas//phyi nas cho ga 'di kun bya//slob mas gsang sngags grub 'dod pas//bla ma mchod pa bya ba'i phyir//slob ma dam tshig bsrung ba yis//gos dang gser dang bdag nyid dang //bu dang bdog 
163b7(rgyud): pa thams cad ni//bla ma dag la yon du dbul//de nas bla mas slob ma rnams//ma yengs legs par bkod nas su//shes rab pha rol phyin bklags te//dam tshig 'di la bsgo bar bya//de ring phyin chad khyed rnams kyis//sangs rgyas chos dang dge 
164a1(rgyud): @#//'dun dang //byang chub sems dpa' rnams dang ni//rig sngags gsang sngags tshogs rnams la//dad pas rab tu brten par bya//rtag par phyag rgya chen po la//khyad par du ni mos par bya//dam tshig can dang mdza' bo dang //bla ma la yang gus 
164a2(rgyud): par bya//lha rnams kun la sdang mi bya//dus mtshams dag tu mchod par bya//ston pa gzhan gyi gzhung mi mchod//rtag tu glo bur 'gron mchod bya//srog chags rnams la byams pa'i sems//rab tu brtan pa nye bar gzhag/theg pa la ni dga' rnams kyi//
164a3(rgyud): bsod nams dag la nan tan bskyed//bzlas brjod byed la 'bad pa yis//gsang sngags spyod la brtson par bya//gsang sngags rgyud las bstan pa yi//dam tshig rnams kyang bsrung bar bya//dam tshig med pa rnams la ni//sngags dang phyag rgya mi sbyin no//gsang 
164a4(rgyud): sngags rgyud ni legs bsrung zhing //de yang bdag gis rtogs par bya//de ltar dam tshig bsgo nas ni//nyid kyi phyag rgya rnams bstan la//nyid kyi rigs kyi rig pa sbyin//dkyil 'khor dag kyang bshad par bya//de nas kha lag bkrus nas su//lha la mchod yon phul 
164a5(rgyud): nas ni//go rims bzhin du thams cad la//bdug spos cho ga bzhin du dbul//de nas dkyil 'khor rig sngags kyis//sbyin sreg brgya rtsa brgyad du dbul//zhi ba'i sngags kyis brgya rtsa brgyad//snying po yis ni nyi shu gcig/ji ltar bstan pa'i gsang sngags kyis//
164a6(rgyud): mar gyis sbyin sreg so sor bya//de yi 'og tu rang sngags kyis//sbyin sreg ji ltar 'dod par bya//de nas phyogs kyi gtor ma sbyin//bsdam zhing kha lag yang bkrus nas//me tog dri la sogs pa yis//slob mas ji ltar 'dod par mchod//slob ma dang bcas ma yengs 
164a7(rgyud): sam//mgo bos gus par phyag 'tshal te//snod du bshad pa gang yin pa//de nas spos chu phul nas su//so so'i sngags kyis ci rigs par//de nas slar ni gshegs su gsol//dman ma rnams ni phyag nas su//mtsho chen nang du dor bar bya//bza' ba dang ni 
164b1(rgyud): bca' ba rnams//ci dgar phongs pa rnams la sbyin//khyi dang bya rog la sogs la//cung zad tsam yang mi sbyin no//yo byad rdzas rnams thams cad ni//slob dpon dag gis bsdus nas su//bdag rang nyid kyis spyad par bya//slob ma rnams la bgor mi rung //dkyil 
164b2(rgyud): 'khor gyi ni yo byad kun//slob dpon bdag nyid dbang bar bshad//de nas slob mas spyad na ni//dam tshig las ni nyams par 'gyur//gang zhig spyod par mi 'dod na//de yi cho ga rim 'di'o//dkon mchog gsum gyi yo byad du//de dag cho ga bzhin du dbul//
164b3(rgyud): gdugs dang rnga yang dril bu sogs//sangs rgyas nyid la dbul bar bya//gdan dang bdug spos dri la sogs//dam pa'i chos la dbul bar bya//gos dang yo byad snod la sogs//dge 'dun rnams la dbul bar bya//de dag la ni slob ma yis//cung zad 
164b4(rgyud): tsam yang mi bkur ro//dge 'dun med na dge slong sbyin//yang na dge slong ma la sbyin//gal te bdag nyid mi 'dod na//dge bsnyen dag la sbyin par bya//de nas de yi phyi de nyin//nyes byas lhag ma zhi ba'i phyir//mkhas pa yis ni zhi ba yi//sbyin sreg brgya rtsa 
164b5(rgyud): brgyad bya'o//de nas gzhan yang rgyud kun las//dkyil 'khor ji ltar rgyas bshad pa'i//cho ga lhag ma lus pa dag/'dir ni lhag par rab tu bshad//slob dpon gsang sngags rgyud la mkhas//snying rje'i bdag nyid tshul khrims ldan//dkyil 'khor bri ba 
164b6(rgyud): la mkhas shing //drang la gtsang sbra che ba shis//khang stong rdo thal gyis byugs pa//gtsang ma bzang po yangs pa dang //lha khang du yang dkyil 'khor ni//ji skad bshad pa legs par bri//ji ltar chu na spyod pa yi//khang khyim byed pa de bzhin du//chu steng spang legs bzang 
164b7(rgyud): po yis//bsdams pa dag tu bri bar bya//mchod sbyin sa dang grong gis//mi gtsang ba yis gang ba dang //gzhan du dkyil 'khor du spyad pa'i//sa ni 'bad de spang bar bya//la la dkyil 'khor bdag po ni//gcig pu kho nar las byar bsgrags//de nyid 
165a1(rgyud): @#//kyis ni bya ba ste//sa 'og dkyil 'khor lta bu yin//kha cig tu ni 'khor tshogs kyis//las rnams bya ba dag tu bshad//de yang de nyid kyis byas te//khro bo'i dkyil 'khor lta bu yin/gang du las ni yod 'gyur la//rang gi las 
165a2(rgyud): kyi cho ga med//der ni mkhas pas spyi rgyud las//gsungs pa'i las rnams bya ba yin//dkyil 'khor du ni dgod pa'i sngags//gang dag yongs su bsgrags pa ni//thog mar de dag re re la//bzlas brjod khri khri bya bar bshad//mkhas pas dkyil 
165a3(rgyud): 'khor gang bris pa//de dag rgyas par bzlas nas ni//dran par bya ba'i phyir yang ni//glegs tham der yang nye bar bzhag/khrus la sogs pa byed pa'i tshe//dkyil 'khor lta ba gzhan bzhag ste//dkyil 'khor 'bri ba'i tshe na ni//nam yang steng par 
165a4(rgyud): mi gzhag go//thig 'debs dus la bab pa'i tshe//slob ma gal te med na yang //de ni phur bu cho ga yis//bdag rang nyid kyis thig gdab pa//dkyil 'khor dag gi las dag la//gal te grogs shig med na yang //der ni mkhas pa bdag nyid 
165a5(rgyud): kyis//rnam pa de bzhin las bya'o//nang bu can dang tshul nyams dang //gtsang sbra chung zhing ma bslabs pa//de lta bu dag rgyud mthong yang //grogs su gzung bar mi bya'o//bder gshegs chos ni mi byed pa//yal rigs btsun dang cing drang //dkyil 'khor 
165a6(rgyud): thos na dad 'gyur te//gal te 'phral la 'ongs na ni//de yi dad pa brtan shes nas//dkyil 'khor dag ni bri ba nas kyang //bdag gis bstan la gzhug pa'i phyir//de ni gcig pu gzhug par bya//de yi sta gon gnas pa yang //de dag nyid ni mod la bya//
165a7(rgyud): slob ma nang mi mthun pa dang //cho ga'i tshul ni med pa la//ji skad bshad pa'i zhi ba bya//rgyal po'i mi ni dpa' snying can//lag na mtshon thogs pas bsrung zhing //sa yi bdag pos gnang nas ni//slob dpon gyis ni dkyil 'khor bri//slob ma yongs su gzung 
165b1(rgyud): ba gang //sta gon gnas tshe med na ni//de yi gzugs brnyan byas nas su//ji skad bshad pa'i las rnams bya//slong ma yongs su gzung ba gang //gal te rkyen 'ga' zhig gis ni//der ni 'ongs par ma gyur na//gzhan dag de yi skyin par gzhug/
165b2(rgyud): sta gon gnas par byas pa las//gal te rkyen gyis ma 'ongs na//bla ma blo ldan mkhas pa yis//der ni slob ma gzhan mi gzung //sta gon gnas par byas pa yas//bla ma gal te dkyil 'khor du//gang zhig 'ongs par ma gyur na/nad kyis btab nas ris par 
165b3(rgyud): 'chi//rkyen 'ga' zhig gis bskal nas su//gal te nyin gcig par chad na//de yi nyin par zhi byas nas//slar yang sta gon gnas par bya//yo byad gces pa ma 'byor yang //bshol ba dag ni yong mi bya//gal te bshol na bla ma la//gnod pa mang po 
165b4(rgyud): 'byung bar 'gyur//dkyil 'khor 'dri ba'i tshe na ni//gnod pa 'ga' zhig 'byung 'gyur na//bshad pa'i sngags kyi stobs kyis su//de ni slar yang bzlog par bya//des kyang bzlog par ma nus na//mchod pa'i yo byad thams cad ni//bsang gtor byas te 
165b5(rgyud): phul nas su//mchod yon phul te slar gshegs gsol//blo dang ldan pas phyi de nyin//dkyil 'khor snga ma bzhin du bri//yang na mkhas pas sbyin sreg gi//las kyis zhi bar rab tu bya//ma bdun gyi ni dkyil 'khor dang //gnod sbyin dkyil 'khor bshad 
165b6(rgyud): pa gang //de la sogs pa dgod pa ni//nyes pa rim bzhin brtag mi dgos//de dag tu ni de bzhin du//dkyil 'khor dgod pa rgyas par bya//cho gar byas pa gzhan dag las//cho ga 'di ni ci rigs sbyar//mdor bsdus bshad pa gang yin dang //rgyas par

166a1(rgyud): @#//mdo las ni//ci bshad cho ga rgyas par bya//bla mas ji skad bstan pa dang //rtog pa dag las ci gsungs dang //gsang sngags kun gyi 'dul ba las//cho ga rim pa ci bshad pa//dkyil 'khor gyi ni cho ga la//mkhas pas 
166a2(rgyud): cho ga de bzhin sbyar//gsang sngags kyi ni tshad dag las//bum pa la sogs bstan byas pa//der yang cho ga 'di yis ni//lhag par byas la nyes pa med//de bzhin gsang sngags cho ga la//lha gzhan gyi ni gtor ma yang //dkyil 'khor dag tu bshad 
166a3(rgyud): pa las//lhag par byas kyang nyes pa med//de bas gang du cung zad cig/bklags te bshad pa gang yin pa//der ni dogs pa med sems kyis//thams cad de bzhin kho nar bya//la lar slob ma dang bcas te//bdug spos dang ni mar me dag/
166a4(rgyud): lag tu thogs te mnyam par ni//dkyil 'khor bskor bar bshad pa dang //dam tshig ston tshe slob ma rnams//las kyi grwar ni zhugs nas su//me tog 'dor bar bya'o zhes//khyad par bshad pa gang yin dang //de la sogs pa'i cho ga dag/
166a5(rgyud): gang du cung zad bshad pa ni//der ni dga' mgu nyid du bya'i//kun la de kun sbyar mi bya//lha yi rigs rnams mthong ba las//dbang bskur ba ni rnam pa bzhi//bla ma mnyam par bzhag pa yis//de dag shes nas ci rigs bya//slob dpon go 
166a6(rgyud): 'phang thob pa'i phyir//dang po yongs su bsgrags pa yin//rig sngags rnams ni bsgrub bya'i phyir//gnyis pa legs par bshad pa yin//bgegs rnams rab tu gzhom pa'i phyir//gsum pa rab tu bsgrags pa yin//bzhi pa 'byor pa thob pa'i phyir//
166a7(rgyud): cho ga rgyas pa de bshad do//dang po cho ga rgyas par ni//nga yis thog mar bshad pa yin//rigs gsum dbang bskur cho ga yang //mdor bsdus nas ni 'dir bshad do//slob ma lha yi rigs mthong ba//rig sngags gang la zlos 'dod pa//de yis brgya rtsa brgyad 
166b1(rgyud): bzlas pa'i//bum pas mngon par dbang bskur bya//de nyid kyis kyang sbyin sreg bya//de nyid du ni de yang bzlas//de ltar gang zhig dbang bskur ba//dbang bskur cho ga gnyis pa yin//sems ni bgegs kyis brlams pa dag/zhi bar bya ba gang 
166b2(rgyud): yin pa//de ni dbang bskur gsum pa ste//phan dang bde ba thob par bshad//dpal la sogs pa'i dkyil 'khor du//rgyas srid thob phyir dbang bskur dang //byur ni med par bya ba dang //dpal yang bskyed par bya ba dang //nor dang bu tsha 'phan pa dang //
166b3(rgyud): de la sogs pa dbang bskur gang //de ni bzhi pa'i mi rnams la//'byor pa sbyin par shes par bya//dbang bskur dang po thob pa ni//sangs rgyas rnams kyang dgongs par 'gyur//de'i sras gsang sngags spyod pa la//brtson pa rnams kyis bkur bar 
166b4(rgyud): 'gyur//rig sngags gsang sngags mang po dang //'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang dang //dpa' bo rdo rje 'dzin pa dag/nyid kyis byin gyis brlabs par 'gyur//de yis sbyar na sgrub pa po//las rnams thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//de ni 
166b5(rgyud): cis kyang mi tshugs shing //lha rnams kyis kyang mchod par 'gyur//'khor ba na ni 'khor ba na//de ni ngan song ltung mi 'gyur//yan lag nyams dang dbul ba dang //smad pa rnams su skye mi 'gyur//tshe rabs dran zhing longs spyod che//tshul khrims ldan

221b1(rgyud): po bzhi dag kyang //sgo sgo dag tu yongs su gzugs//khor yug rnam par rgyal ba yi//ba dan sna tshogs rnams kyis brgyan//rta babs dag kyang rgyal mtshan dang //lo ma sar pa dag gis brgyan//phreng ba brgyus pa gshibs pa dag/blo dang ldan pas gcad par 
221b2(rgyud): bya//phyi rol kho ra khor yug tu//mar gyi mar me gsal bya zhing //bdug spos rigs ni sna tshogs pas//rgyun mi 'chad par bdug par bya//dkyil 'khor gnyis pa khor yug tu//khru bdun dag dang ldan pa dang //gsum pa khru ni gsum pa ste//cho 
221b3(rgyud): ga de ltar yongs su gzung //mchod sogs cho ga gang yin dang //dkyil 'khor cho ga sngon bzhin bya//gzhan yang cho ga lhag par ni//sbyin sreg dag kyang bya bar gsungs//dbus su rang gi rig sngags kyis//bum pa gang ba bzhag nas su//de yi phyogs 
221b4(rgyud): bzhi dag tu yang //sbyin sreg cho ga bzhin du bya//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs rnams kun//shar gyi phyogs su bri bar bya//byang phyogs su ni pad+ma'i rigs//cha dang mthun par legs par bri//mkhas pas lho yi phyogs su ni//rdo rje'i rigs kun bri bar bya//
221b5(rgyud): nub kyi phyogs su drag po dang //gcig pu gnod sbyin rigs rnams bri//de yi lhag pa'i cha dag kyang //nang rim bzhin du phyi rol bri//de yi phyi rol 'jig rten skyong //gzhan dag kyang ni bya bar bsgrags//gsang sngags zlos pas dang por ni//bdag po lnga 
221b6(rgyud): rnams dgod par bya//de nas rig sngags rgyal po dang //rig sngags rgyal mor bsgrags pa dang //las kun byed pas gsang sngags dang //dkyil 'khor ngag tu gang bstan pa'i//pho nya che phra go rims bzhin//dkyil 'khor du ni dgod par bya//sgo kar 
221b7(rgyud): bgegs kyi sde dpon dang //khro bo bdud rtsi thab sbyor dang //lha mo gzhan gyis mi thub bzhag/lhag ma ci dgar bya bar bshad//de ltar rgyud don mthong ba yis//dkyil 'khor dgod par byas nas kyang //der ni snying po spyan drangs te//cho ga lhag ma'ang bya bar 
222a1(rgyud): @#//gsungs//mkhas pas phyogs bzhi rigs re la//sbyin sreg cho ga bzhin byas nas//nub kyi bum pa'i drung du ni//rang gi rig sngags sbyin sreg bya//'dir ni sbyin sreg cho ga bzhin//byas na thams cad tshim par 'gyur//der ni gtor ma 
222a2(rgyud): rgya cher bya//des ni lha rnams mgu bar 'gyur//dkyil 'khor 'di ni bris nas kyang //bzlas brjod sbyin sreg ci dgar bya//rang rigs rig pas rig sngags la//mar gyi sbyin sreg dag kyang bya//'o thug mar dang sbyar ba'am//til 
222a3(rgyud): gsum sbyar ba yis//mtshan mo'i lhag ma rang rigs kyi//rig pas rig sngags sbyin sreg bya//las 'di dag gis nges par yang //lha nyid myur du 'grub 'gyur zhing //tshim par 'gyur zhing mnyes par 'gyur//legs par yang ni skong yang 
222a4(rgyud): 'gyur//dkyil 'khor 'di na bris nas kyang //gsang sngags rjes mthun sbyin sreg bya//lan bdun kho na bris nas kyang //the tshom med par nges par 'grub//rig pa'i rgyal po'i rig sngags sam//de bzhin rigs kyi rig sngags kyis//rig sngags 
222a5(rgyud): dag la drag spyad pa//thams cad btud de bya bar gsungs//spra tshil las ni gzugs byas nas//de yang rig sngags kyis bzlas te//drag shul spyod pa'i sbyin sreg ni//las kyi cho ga thams cad bya//ske tshe yungs mar kyis blugs na//rims 
222a6(rgyud): nad drag pos 'debs par 'gyur//kha sbub tu ni bzhag byas na//lus la sdug bsngal tshor ba 'byung //mi mthun pa yis brdeg pa dang //me tog gis kyang bsnun par bya//de nyid la ni spyo bar gsungs//tho 'tsham pa yang ci dgar bya//dkyil 'khor 
222a7(rgyud): 'dir ni gsang sngags dag/gdon bzhin du ni tshar bcad na//cho ga las ni bstan pa yi//dngos grub dag kyang stsol bar 'gyur//'dir ni lha nyid mngon gshegs nas//nyid kyis bsgrub pa nyid la ni//dngos grub myur du sbyin par 'gyur//byin 
222b1(rgyud): nas zhi ba'i cho ga bya//gal te ci zhig ltar gyur na//dug khrag lan tshwa ske tshe dang //yungs kar sbyar nas dkyil 'khor 'dir//mtshan ma lhag ma bsreg par byas//rang gi rig sngags lha nyid ni//sdug bsngal bzhin du myur gshegs nas//kye kye 
222b2(rgyud): nga yis mchog sbyin zhes//sgrub pa po la gcugs par mdzad//gal te bag med ma byon na//sgrub pa pos ni zhag gsum du//'jigs pa med pas rang gi sha//sreg blugs gsum ni der bsregs na//de tshe nges par mngon gshegs 
222b3(rgyud): nas//sgrub pa po la chags mdzad cing //ci 'dod pa yi dngos grub stsol//nyes pa ma lus brjod par mdzad//mtshams med lnga ni byed pas kyang //zhag dgur las 'di nges byas na//gdon mi za bar 'grub 'gyur bas//
222b4(rgyud): de phyir legs par brtsam par bya//las 'di don yod bya ba'i phyir//rig sngags dag la rgol ba'i tshe//zlos pa 'jigs pa med pa dang //bsrung ba dag kyang byas te bya//'dir ni dngos grub legs byin na//cho ga bzhin du zhi bar bya//
222b5(rgyud): las kyi nyes pa ci yod pa//de dag rab tu bsgrims te gshegs//gsang sngags dngos grub thams cad la//dkyil 'khor 'di ni dam pa yin//sbyin sreg las la bstan pa yi//sbyin sreg rnam gsum bya bar gsungs//las rnams thams 
222b6(rgyud): cad byas na ni//dngos grub rnam gsum thob par 'gyur//zlos pa rang gi rig sngags dag/gdon bzhin tshar gcod byed pa na//nyin gcig bzhin du yo byad ni//shin tu sar pa dag gis bya//mkhas pas bsrung ba byas pa dag/bag yod pa la gtsang ba dang //

223a1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du/d+h+yA not+tA ra pa Ta la kra ma/bod skad du/bsam gtan gyi phyi ma rim par phye ba/dpal rdo rje rnon po la phyag 'tshal lo//gsang sngags rgyud kyi phyogs la yang //gang gi blo ni mi zhen pa//de yang smra bar spro ba ni//ma smras na ni mi

223a3(rgyud): yang //sngags las dngos grub mi 'thob bo//lung dang man ngag ma lus pa//gang gis yongs su spangs pa dag/grub pa myong ba la la zhig/yod dam de ni med pa smros//de nas gsang sngags bdag nyid che//dngos grub rlabs ni 'khrigs pa can//bsam

223a5(rgyud): grub snod//de dag legs par mi shes na//khyod kyis dngos grub ci zhig 'thob//rig sngags las ni grub pa po//gzhan nam gzhan min ci zhig nyes//gal te gzhan na 'grub mi 'gyur//'on te gzhan min grub pa nyid//gal te gzhan min khyod 
223a6(rgyud): 'grub pas//mkha' la sogs par da song zhig/de ste min na brdzun tshig pas//pa lang rdzi dang lhan cig smros//mi 'chi tshangs chen pha mchod kyis//yongs su bsams nas tshig smras pa//mi mjed bdag pos gang smras pa//gzhan pas bdag la dngos grub med//rigs 
223a7(rgyud): las grub pa gzhan min na//dngos grub bdag la yod dam ci//de ltar gyur na ci zhig nyes//'on tang 'gal ba ci zhig yod//e ma'o tshangs pa khyod kyi blo//shin tu dri ma med pa dang //shes rab gsal bar ngas bsams na//tshig re la ni rnyogs pa bzhin//gzhan pas khyod 
223b1(rgyud): la dngos grub med//gzhan min pa ni dngos grub rgyu//gzhan min pa las grub 'dod na//smyon pa nyid ni yin par 'gyur//'gro ba'i bdag po ji ltar na//dngos grub yod par khas 'che ba//ye shes sgron mar gyur pa khyod//ji ltar phan pa bshad du gsol//bdag gis rnam
